result.append(FactorTemplateEntry.model_validate(entry).model_dump())\n        elif hasattr(entry, \"model_dump\"):\n result\n\n\ndef _normalize_option_template_entries(\n    entries: list[OptionTemplateEntry] | list[dict[str, Any]] | None,\n) -> list[dict[str, Any]] | None:\n    \"\"\"Coerce option template entries        if \"alternative_title\" not in raw and \"option_title\" in raw:\n                raw[\"alternative_title\"] = raw.pop(\"option_title\")\n            if \"alternative_description\" not in raw result.append(OptionTemplateEntry.model_validate(raw).model_dump())\n        elif hasattr(entry, \"model_dump\"):\n            result.append(OptionTemplateEntry.model_validate(entry.model_dump()).model_dump())\n        else:\n            result.append(OptionTemplateEntry.model_validate(dict(entry)).model_dump())\n    return result\n\n\nclass CustomerFactorTemplates(SQLModel, table=True):\n    \"\"\"Project/option/factor templates (table name kept for compatibility).\n\n    Import is always a one-time copy into a project   sa_column=Column(JSONB, default=None, nullable=True),\n        description=\"List of option entry dicts (alternative_title, alternative_description)\",\n    )\n    use_with_projects: bool = Field(default=False, description=\"Pick one mode when imported at project scope (true); rank all when false\")\n    private_participation: bool = Field(default=False, description=\"Private create_factor_template(self, session: Session, usr_context: UserContext) -> CustomerFactorTemplateResult_One:\n        if self.customer_id != usr_context.sess_user.customer_id:\n            log_id = CustomerFactorTemplateResult_One(failure_reason=f\"Internal failure - Customer ID mismatch\", log_id=log_id)\n        if self.enable_global_share and usr_context.sess_user.system_user_mode < 2:\n            log_id = log_event(\"create_factor_template: enable_global_share requires system admin\", usr_context=usr_context, severity=3)\n            return CustomerFactorTemplateResult_One(failure_reason=\"Not allowed; system user required to enable global share\", log_id=log_id)\n        if self.enable_global_share:\n            log_event(\n self._normalize_payload()\n        session.add(self)\n        session.commit()\n        session.refresh(self)\n        return CustomerFactorTemplateResult_One(factor_template_info=self)\n\n    def return CustomerFactorTemplateResult_One(failure_reason=\"Internal failure - Customer ID mismatch\", log_id=log_id)\n        if self.enable_global_share and usr_context.sess_user.system_user_mode < session.commit()\n        session.refresh(self)\n        return CustomerFactorTemplateResult_One(factor_template_info=self)\n\n    def update_factor_template(\n        self,\n        session:     session.close()\n            return existing_result\n        existing = existing_result.factor_template_info\n        failure_reason, log_id, existing = v for k, v in update_fields.items()\n                if k not in ('id', 'customer_id', 'create_date', 'factor_template_id', 'modify_date', 'deleted_date')\n            }\n        if 'enable_global_share' in update_fields:\n            new_share = bool(update_fields['enable_global_share'])\n            if new_share != bool(existing.enable_global_share):\n                if usr_context.sess_user.system_user_mode < 2:\n                    if clear_lock:\n                        session.close()\n                    log_id = log_event(\n         return CustomerFactorTemplateResult_One(\n                        failure_reason=\"Not allowed; system user required to change global share\",\n                        log_id=log_id,\n CustomerFactorTemplateDeleteResult(success=False, failure_reason=existing_result.failure_reason or \"not found\", factor_template_id=self.id, log_id=existing_result.log_id)\n        existing = existing_result.factor_template_info\n        failure_reason, log_id, existing = CustomerFactorTemplates.check_user_access(\n            'delete_factor_template', key=self.id, usr_context=usr_context, check_row=existing, update_mode=True\n        )\n        if existing is None or failure_reason != '':\n            return CustomerFactorTemplateDeleteResult(failure_reason=failure_reason, log_id=log_id, factor_template_id=self.id)\n        existing.deleted_date = datetime.now()\n        session.add(existing)\n        session.commit()\n        return CustomerFactorTemplateDeleteResult(success=True, factor_template_id=existing.id)\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def get_factor_template_by_id(session: Session, *, id: int, usr_context: UserContext, clear_lock: bool = True) -> CustomerFactorTemplateResult_One:\n        row = session.exec(select(CustomerFactorTemplates).where(\n            CustomerFactorTemplates.id == id,\n            CustomerFactorTemplates.deleted_date == None,  # noqa: E711\n        ).limit(1)).one_or_none()\n        # Allow read of global templates from other tenants\n        if row is not None and row.enable_global_share:\n            failure_reason, log_id = '', 0\n        else:\n            failure_reason, log_id, row = CustomerFactorTemplates.check_user_access(\n                'get_factor_template_by_id', key=id, usr_context=usr_context, check_row=row, update_mode=False\n            )\n        if clear_lock:\n            session.close()\n        return CustomerFactorTemplateResult_One(failure_reason=failure_reason, log_id=log_id, factor_template_info=row)\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def get_all_for_customer(session: Session, *, usr_context: UserContext, clear_lock: bool = True) -> CustomerFactorTemplateResult_Many:\n        rows = list(session.exec(select(CustomerFactorTemplates).where(\n            CustomerFactorTemplates.customer_id == usr_context.sess_user.customer_id,\n            CustomerFactorTemplates.deleted_date == None,  # noqa: E711\n        )).all())\n        if clear_lock:\n            session.close()\n        return CustomerFactorTemplateResult_Many(factor_template_info_list=rows)\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def get_available_templates(\n        session: Session,\n        *,\n        usr_context: UserContext,\n        include_global: bool = True,\n        for_use: str | None = None,\n        clear_lock: bool = True,\n    ) -> CustomerFactorTemplateResult_Many:\n        customer_id = usr_context.sess_user.customer_id\n        if include_global:\n            rows = list(session.exec(select(CustomerFactorTemplates).where(\n                CustomerFactorTemplates.deleted_date == None,  # noqa: E711\n                CustomerFactorTemplates.disabled == False,  # noqa: E712\n                or_(\n                    CustomerFactorTemplates.customer_id == customer_id,\n                    CustomerFactorTemplates.enable_global_share == True,  # noqa: E712\n                ),\n            )).all())\n        else:\n            rows = list(session.exec(select(CustomerFactorTemplates).where(\n                CustomerFactorTemplates.customer_id == customer_id,\n                CustomerFactorTemplates.deleted_date == None,  # noqa: E711\n                CustomerFactorTemplates.disabled == False,  # noqa: E712\n            )).all())\n        if for_use == 'projects':\n            rows = [r for r in rows if r.use_with_projects]\n        elif for_use == 'options':\n            rows = [r for r in rows if r.use_with_options]\n        elif for_use == 'factors':\n            rows = [r for r in rows if r.use_with_factors]\n        if clear_lock:\n            session.close()\n        return CustomerFactorTemplateResult_Many(factor_template_info_list=rows)\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def check_user_access(action: str, *, key: Any, usr_context: UserContext, check_row: CustomerFactorTemplates | None, update_mode: bool) -> tuple[str, int, CustomerFactorTemplates | None]:\n        failure_reason = ''\n        log_id = 0\n        if check_row is None:\n            log_id = log_event(log_message=f'{action} - Template {key}: NOT FOUND', severity=2, usr_context=usr_context)\n            return 'Template not found', log_id, None\n        if usr_context.sess_user.system_user_mode < 2 and check_row.customer_id != usr_context.sess_user.customer_id:\n            log_id = log_event(log_message=f'{action} - Template {key}: customer mismatch', severity=3, usr_context=usr_context)\n            return \"Not allowed; system user mismatch\", log_id, None\n        if update_mode and usr_context.sess_user.customer_admin is False and usr_context.sess_user.system_user_mode < 2:\n            log_id = log_event(log_message=f'{action} - Template {key}: non-admin', severity=3, usr_context=usr_context)\n            return \"Not allowed; non-admin\", log_id, None\n        return failure_reason, log_id, check_row\n\n\nclass CustomerProjectAlternatives(SQLModel, table=True):\n    id: int | None = Field(default=None, primary_key=True)\n    customer_id: int = Field(index=True, unique=False)\n    project_id: int = Field(index=True, unique=False, description=\"Project this option (alternative entry) belongs to\")\n    alternative_title: str | None = Field(default=None, description=\"Title of the option (API field name: alternative_title)\")\n    alternative_description: str | None = Field(default=None, description=\"Description of the option (API field name: alternative_description)\")\n    disabled: bool = Field(default=False, description=\"When true, option is excluded from comparisons\")\n    create_date: datetime = Field(sa_column=Column(DateTime, default=func.now()))\n    modify_date: datetime = Field(sa_column=Column(DateTime, default=func.now(), onupdate=func.now()))\n    deleted_date: datetime | None = Field(default=None, description=\"Date entry was deleted; if not None, entry is considered deleted and not active\")\n\n    def create_alternative(self, session: Session, usr_context: UserContext) -> CustomerProjectAlternativeResult_One:\n        project = CustomerProject.get_customer_project_by_id_system(\n            session=session, customer_id=usr_context.sess_user.customer_id if usr_context.sess_user.system_user_mode < 2 else self.customer_id or usr_context.sess_user.customer_id,\n            id=self.project_id, clear_lock=False\n        )\n        if project is None and usr_context.sess_user.system_user_mode >= 2:\n            project = session.exec(select(CustomerProject).where(\n                CustomerProject.id == self.project_id,\n                CustomerProject.deleted_date == None,  # noqa: E711\n            ).limit(1)).one_or_none()\n        if project is None:\n            log_id = log_event(f\"create_alternative: project {self.project_id} {"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787340111280,"sessionID":"ses_fda3a7ac1ffeJaCEkE5n11aTw9","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"read","callID":"call_01a025c5ba4d7d61918754f68d6b1050","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/utils/ai/prompts.py"},"output":"<path>/workspaces/powerchoice/src/utils/ai/prompts.py</path>\n<type>file</type>\n<content>\n1: from __future__ import annotations\n2: \n3: import math\n4: from typing import Any\n5: \n6: from ..comparison_question import normalize_comparison_question\n7: \n8: # --- quota helpers -----------------------------------------------------------\n9: \n10: def ai_vote_threshold(group_size: int) -> int:\n11:     \"\"\"Combined TIE+SKIP quota before prompt restricts to 1-or-2 only.\n12: \n13:     Spec: 20% of group size with minimum 1. Group size is the number of\n14:     items in the sort group (len(item_ids_initial)). Once quota is reached,\n15:     the choice line switches to \"Respond with Option 1 or Option 2.\"\n16: \n17:     Quota is per-group, combined TIE+SKIP. Threshold 0 only for n<=0\n18:     (return 0 so caller can disallow abstention entirely).\n19:     \"\"\"\n20:     try:\n21:         n = int(group_size or 0)\n22:     except (TypeError, ValueError):\n23:         n = 0\n24:     if n <= 0:\n25:         return 0\n26:     ratio = 0.2\n27:     min_q = 1\n28:     try:\n29:         from ...config.config_settings import settings as _s\n30:         ratio = float(getattr(_s, \"AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_RATIO\", 0.2) or 0.2)\n31:         min_q = int(getattr(_s, \"AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_MIN\", 1) or 1)\n32:     except Exception:\n33:         pass\n34:     # also try pvf/runtime overrides if present (mirrors config.py pattern)\n35:     try:\n36:         from ...pvf.bindings.pvf_invocation import runtime_settings\n37:         rs = runtime_settings()\n38:         if rs is not None:\n39:             if hasattr(rs, \"AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_RATIO\"):\n40:                 ratio = float(getattr(rs, \"AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_RATIO\") or ratio)\n41:             if hasattr(rs, \"AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_MIN\"):\n42:                 min_q = int(getattr(rs, \"AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_MIN\") or min_q)\n43:     except Exception:\n44:         pass\n45:     ratio = max(0.01, min(0.9, float(ratio)))\n46:     min_q = max(1, int(min_q))\n47:     return max(min_q, math.ceil(n * ratio))\n48: \n49: \n50: # alias for clarity — spec wording\n51: tie_skip_quota = ai_vote_threshold\n52: \n53: \n54: def combined_abstentions(answers: list[Any] | None) -> int:\n55:     \"\"\"Count combined skip+tie (inclusive of 'unsure') in a group.\"\"\"\n56:     if not answers:\n57:         return 0\n58:     cnt = 0\n59:     for a in answers:\n60:         resp = getattr(a, \"response\", None) if not isinstance(a, dict) else a.get(\"response\")\n61:         if resp is None:\n62:             continue\n63:         r = str(resp).lower()\n64:         if r in (\"skipped\", \"skip\", \"tie\", \"unsure\"):\n65:             cnt += 1\n66:     return cnt\n67: \n68: \n69: def quota_reached(group_size: int, abstentions: int) -> bool:\n70:     thresh = ai_vote_threshold(group_size)\n71:     if thresh <= 0:\n72:         return True  # no abstentions allowed for degenerate groups\n73:     return int(abstentions or 0) >= thresh\n74: \n75: \n76: # --- template resolution + safe formatting -----------------------------------\n77: \n78: _DEFAULT_CHOICE_TIE_SKIP = \"Respond with 1, 2, TIE or SKIP. Use TIE and SKIP sparingly.\"\n79: _DEFAULT_CHOICE_STRICT = \"Respond with Option 1 or Option 2.\"\n80: \n81: _DEFAULT_SINGLE = (\n82:     \"You are an agent being asked for a ranking opinion between the following two options. \"\n83:     \"Choose the best overall answer based on information provided and your prior knowledge.\\n\\n\"\n84:     \"Project Context: {project_title}\\n{project_description}\\n\\n\"\n85:     \"Option 1: {option1_title}\\n{option1_description}\\n\\n\"\n86:     \"Option 2: {option2_title}\\n{option2_description}\\n\\n{choice_line}\"\n87: )\n88: \n89: _DEFAULT_FACTOR_OPTION = (\n90:     \"You are an agent being asked for a ranking opinion between the following two options. \"\n91:     \"This ranking is for a specific factor or perspective. Make a selection based on the factor \"\n92:     \"and specific question, keeping the factor in context, based on your overall knowledge.\\n\\n\"\n93:     \"Project Context: {project_title}\\n{project_description}\\n\\n\"\n94:     \"Current Factor: {factor_title}\\n{factor_description}\\n\\n{factor_question}\\n\\n\"\n95:     \"Option 1: {option1_title}\\n{option1_description}\\n\\n\"\n96:     \"Option 2: {option2_title}\\n{option2_description}\\n\\n{choice_line}\"\n97: )\n98: \n99: _DEFAULT_FACTOR_RANKING = (\n100:     \"You are an agent being asked for a ranking the importance of two factors being used in a \"\n101:     \"weighted selection ranking process. Choose the more important factor based on information \"\n102:     \"provided and your prior knowledge.\\n\\n\"\n103:     \"Project Context: {project_title}\\n{project_description}\\n\\n\"\n104:     \"Factor 1: {factor1_title}\\nFactor 1 Question Format: {factor1_question}\\nFactor 1 description: {factor1_description}\\n\\n\"\n105:     \"Factor 2: {factor2_title}\\nFactor 2 Question Format: {factor2_question}\\nFactor 2 description: {factor2_description}\\n\\n{choice_line}\"\n106: )\n107: \n108: \n109: def _get_templates() -> tuple[str, str, str, str, str]:\n110:     \"\"\"Return (single_tpl, factor_opt_tpl, factor_rank_tpl, choice_tie_skip, choice_strict) from settings.\"\"\"\n111:     single = _DEFAULT_SINGLE\n112:     factor_opt = _DEFAULT_FACTOR_OPTION\n113:     factor_rank = _DEFAULT_FACTOR_RANKING\n114:     tie_skip = _DEFAULT_CHOICE_TIE_SKIP\n115:     strict = _DEFAULT_CHOICE_STRICT\n116:     try:\n117:         from ...config.config_settings import settings as app_s\n118:         single = str(getattr(app_s, \"AI_PROMPT_SINGLE_OPTION_TEMPLATE\", single) or single)\n119:         factor_opt = str(getattr(app_s, \"AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_OPTION_TEMPLATE\", factor_opt) or factor_opt)\n120:         factor_rank = str(getattr(app_s, \"AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_RANKING_TEMPLATE\", factor_rank) or factor_rank)\n121:         tie_skip = str(getattr(app_s, # runtime override (covers pvf invocation startup values if injected)\n126:     try:\n127:         from ...pvf.bindings.pvf_invocation import runtime_settings\n128:         rs = runtime_settings()\n129:         if rs is not None:\n130:             if hasattr(rs, \"AI_PROMPT_SINGLE_OPTION_TEMPLATE\"):\n131:                 v = getattr(rs, \"AI_PROMPT_SINGLE_OPTION_TEMPLATE\")\n132:                 if v:\n133:                     single = str(v)\n134:             if hasattr(rs, \"AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_OPTION_TEMPLATE\"):\n135:                 v = getattr(rs, \"AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_OPTION_TEMPLATE\")\n136:                 if v:\n137:                     factor_opt = str(v)\n138:             if hasattr(rs,          if hasattr(rs, \"AI_PROMPT_CHOICE_TIE_SKIP\"):\n143:                 v = getattr(rs, \"AI_PROMPT_CHOICE_TIE_SKIP\")\n144:                 if v:\n145:                     tie_skip = str(v)\n146:             if hasattr(rs, \"AI_PROMPT_CHOICE_STRICT\"):\n147:                 v = getattr(rs, \"AI_PROMPT_CHOICE_STRICT\")\n148:                 if v:\n149:                     strict = str(v)\n150:     except Exception:\n151:         pass\n152:     return single, factor_opt, factor_rank, tie_skip, strict\n153: \n154: \n155: def _safe_format(template: str, params: dict[str, Any], fallback: str) -> str:\n156:     \"\"\"Format template with params; on failure log and return fallback formatted with same params.\"\"\"\n157:     try:\n158:         return template.format(**params)\n159:     except Exception as ex:\n160:         try:\n161:             from ...pvf.utils import utils_show as ut\n162:             ut.show_vars_semi(\n163:      except Exception:\n173:             # ultimate minimal fallback\n174:             try:\n175:                 from ...pvf.utils import utils_show as ut2\n176:                 ut2.show_vars_semi(\"ai prompt fallback also failed\", error=str(ex)[:200])\n177:             except Exception:\n178:                 pass\n179:             # return template raw with best effort\n180:             return fallback\n181: \n182: \n183: def factor_suggest_messages(*, title: str, description: str) -> list[dict[str, str]]:\n184:     problem_title = (title or \"\").strip()\n185:     problem_description = (description or \"\").strip()\n186:     system = (\n187:         \"You suggest comparison factors for a decision problem. \"\n188:         \"Return JSON only, no markdown. \"\n189:         \"Schema: {\\\"factors\\\": [{\\\"title\\\": string, \\\"description\\\": string}]}. \"\n190:         \"Produce 5 to 8 distinct factors. Titles are short (2-6 words). \"\n191:         \"Descriptions are one or two sentences. Do not include polarity, scores, or rankings.\"\n192:     )\n193:     user = (\n194:         f\"Problem title: {problem_title}\\n\"\n195:         f\"Problem description: {problem_description}\\n\"\n196:         \"Suggest the factors that would most help people compare options.\"\n197:     )\n198:     return [\n199:         {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": system},\n200:         {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": user},\n201:     ]\n202: \n203: \n204: def pairwise_messages(\n205:     *,\n206:     problem_title: str,\n207:     problem_description: str,\n208:     left_title: str,\n209:     left_description: str | None,\n210:     right_title: str,\n211:     right_description: str | None,\n212:     factor_title: str | None,\n213:     factor_description: str | None,\n214:     group_type: str,\n215:     comparison_question: str | None = None,\n216:     allow_skip_tie: bool = True,\n217:     # Never explain.\"\n333:     return [\n334:         {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": system},\n335:         {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": content},\n336:     ]\n337: \n338: \n339: def parse_pairwise_choice(text: str) -> str | None:\n340:     \"\"\"Parse LLM token to left/right/tie/skipped.\n341: \n342:     Accepts: 1 / 2 / Option 1 / Option 2 / LEFT / RIGHT / TIE / SKIP (and synonyms).\n343:     Returns \"left\" for Option 1, \"right\" for Option 2, \"tie\", \"skipped\", or None (unknown).\n344:     \"\"\"\n345:     raw = (text or \"\").strip()\n346:     if not raw:\n347:         return None\n348:     # first token plus full-text checks\n349:     first = raw.split()[0].strip().upper().strip(\".,:;\\\"'`\")\n350:     # numeric / option forms\n351:     if first in (\"1\", \"OPTION1\", \"OPTION_1\"):\n352:         return \"left\"\n353:     if first in (\"2\", \"OPTION2\", \"OPTION_2\"):\n354:         return \"right\"\n355:     # handle \"Option 1\" as two tokens — check lower\n356:     lowered = raw.strip().lower()\n357:     if lowered.startswith(\"option 1\") or lowered.startswith(\"option1\") or lowered == \"1\":\n358:         return \"left\"\n359:     if lowered.startswith(\"option 2\") or lowered.startswith(\"option2\") or lowered == \"2\":\n360:         return \"right\"\n361:     if first in (\"LEFT\", \"L\", \"WINNER_LEFT\", \"A\"):\n362:         return \"left\"\n363:     if first in (\"RIGHT\", \"R\", \"WINNER_RIGHT\", \"B\"):\n364:         return \"right\"\n365:     if first in (\"TIE\", \"EQUAL\", \"DRAW\"):\n366:         return \"tie\"\n367:     if first in (\"SKIP\", \"SKIPPED\", \"UNSURE\", \"PASS\", \"ABSTAIN\"):\n368:         return \"skipped\"\n369:     if lowered.startswith(\"left\"):\n370:         return \"left\"\n371:     if lowered.startswith(\"right\"):\n372:         return \"right\"\n373:     if lowered.startswith(\"tie\"):\n374:         return \"tie\"\n375:     if lowered.startswith(\"skip\") or lowered.startswith(\"unsure\") or lowered.startswith(\"abstain\") or lowered.startswith(\"pass\"):\n376:         return \"skipped\"\n377:     # bare numeric after stripping punctuation\n378:     if first == \"1\":\n379:         return \"left\"\n380:     if first == \"2\":\n381:         return \"right\"\n382:     return None\n383: \n384: \n385: def parse_factor_suggestions(payload: Any) -> list[dict[str, str]]:\n386:     rows: list = []\n387:     if isinstance(payload, dict):\n388:         raw = payload.get(\"factors\") or payload.get(\"suggestions\") or []\n389:         rows = raw if isinstance(raw, list) else []\n390:     elif isinstance(payload, list):\n391:         rows = payload\n392:     out: list[dict[str, str]] = []\n393:     seen: set[str] = set()\n394:     for item in rows:\n395:         if not isinstance(item, dict):\n396:             continue\n397:         title = str(item.get(\"title\") or item.get(\"factor_title\") or \"\").strip()\n398:         description = str(item.get(\"description\") or item.get(\"factor_description\") or \"\").strip()\n399:         if not title:\n400:             continue\n401:         key = title.lower()\n402:         if key in seen:\n403:             continue\n404:         seen.add(key)\n405:         out.append({\"title\": title[:200], \"description\": description[:2000]})\n406:         if len(out) >= 8:\n407:             break\n408:     return out\n\n(End of file - total 408 lines)\n</content>","metadata":{"preview":"from __future__ import annotations\n\nimport math\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom ..comparison_question import normalize_comparison_question\n\n# --- quota helpers -----------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef ai_vote_threshold(group_size: int) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Combined TIE+SKIP quota before prompt restricts to 1-or-2 only.\n\n    Spec: 20% of group size with minimum 1. Group size is the number of\n    items in the sort group (len(item_ids_initial)). Once quota is reached,\n    the choice line switches to \"Respond with Option 1 or Option 2.\"\n\n    Quota is per-group, combined TIE+SKIP. Threshold 0 only for n<=0\n    (return 0 so caller can disallow abstention entirely).\n    \"\"\"\n    try:","truncated":false,"loaded":[],"display":{"type":"file","path":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/utils/ai/prompts.py","text":"from __future__ import annotations\n\nimport math\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom ..comparison_question import normalize_comparison_question\n\n# --- quota helpers -----------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef ai_vote_threshold(group_size: int) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Combined TIE+SKIP quota before prompt restricts to 1-or-2 only.\n\n    Spec: 20% of group size with minimum 1. Group size is the number of\n    items in the sort group (len(item_ids_initial)). Once quota is reached,\n    the choice line switches to \"Respond with Option 1 or Option 2.\"\n\n    Quota is per-group, combined TIE+SKIP. Threshold 0 only for n<=0\n    (return 0 so caller can disallow abstention entirely).\n    \"\"\"\n    try:\n        n = int(group_size or 0)\n    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n        n = 0\n    if n <= 0:\n        return 0\n    ratio = 0.2\n    min_q = 1\n    try:\n        from ...config.config_settings import settings as _s\n        ratio = float(getattr(_s, \"AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_RATIO\", 0.2) or 0.2)\n        min_q = int(getattr(_s, \"AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_MIN\", 1) or 1)\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n    # also try pvf/runtime overrides if present (mirrors config.py pattern)\n    try:\n        from ...pvf.bindings.pvf_invocation import runtime_settings\n        rs = runtime_settings()\n        if rs is not None:\n            if hasattr(rs, \"AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_RATIO\"):\n                ratio = float(getattr(rs, \"AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_RATIO\") or ratio)\n            if hasattr(rs, \"AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_MIN\"):\n                min_q = int(getattr(rs, \"AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_MIN\") or min_q)\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n    ratio = max(0.01, min(0.9, float(ratio)))\n    min_q = max(1, int(min_q))\n    return max(min_q, math.ceil(n * ratio))\n\n\n# alias for clarity — spec wording\ntie_skip_quota = ai_vote_threshold\n\n\ndef combined_abstentions(answers: list[Any] | None) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Count combined skip+tie (inclusive of 'unsure') in a group.\"\"\"\n    if not answers:\n        return 0\n    cnt = 0\n    for a in answers:\n        resp = getattr(a, \"response\", None) if not isinstance(a, dict) else a.get(\"response\")\n        if resp is None:\n            continue\n        r = str(resp).lower()\n        if r in (\"skipped\", \"skip\", \"tie\", \"unsure\"):\n            cnt += 1\n    return cnt\n\n\ndef quota_reached(group_size: int, abstentions: int) -> bool:\n    thresh = ai_vote_threshold(group_size)\n    if thresh <= 0:\n        return True  # no abstentions allowed for degenerate groups\n    return int(abstentions or 0) >= thresh\n\n\n# --- template resolution + safe formatting -----------------------------------\n\n_DEFAULT_CHOICE_TIE_SKIP = \"Respond with 1, 2, TIE or SKIP. Use TIE and SKIP sparingly.\"\n_DEFAULT_CHOICE_STRICT = \"Respond with Option 1 or Option 2.\"\n\n_DEFAULT_SINGLE = (\n    \"You are an agent being asked for a ranking opinion between the following two options. \"\n    \"Choose the best overall answer based on information provided and your prior knowledge.\\n\\n\"\n    \"Project Context: {project_title}\\n{project_description}\\n\\n\"\n    \"Option 1: {option1_title}\\n{option1_description}\\n\\n\"\n    \"Option 2: {option2_title}\\n{option2_description}\\n\\n{choice_line}\"\n)\n\n_DEFAULT_FACTOR_OPTION = (\n    \"You are an agent being asked for a ranking opinion between the following two options. \"\n    \"This ranking is for a specific factor or perspective. Make a selection based on the factor \"\n    \"and specific question, keeping the factor in context, based on your overall knowledge.\\n\\n\"\n    \"Project Context: {project_title}\\n{project_description}\\n\\n\"\n    \"Current Factor: {factor_title}\\n{factor_description}\\n\\n{factor_question}\\n\\n\"\n    \"Option 1: {option1_title}\\n{option1_description}\\n\\n\"\n    \"Option 2: {option2_title}\\n{option2_description}\\n\\n{choice_line}\"\n)\n\n_DEFAULT_FACTOR_RANKING = (\n    \"You are an agent being asked for a ranking the importance of two factors being used in a \"\n    \"weighted selection ranking process. Choose the more important factor based on information \"\n    \"provided and your prior knowledge.\\n\\n\"\n    \"Project Context: {project_title}\\n{project_description}\\n\\n\"\n    \"Factor 1: {factor1_title}\\nFactor 1 Question Format: {factor1_question}\\nFactor 1 description: {factor1_description}\\n\\n\"\n    \"Factor 2: {factor2_title}\\nFactor 2 Question Format: {factor2_question}\\nFactor 2 description: {factor2_description}\\n\\n{choice_line}\"\n)\n\n\ndef _get_templates() -> tuple[str, str, str, str, str]:\n    \"\"\"Return (single_tpl, factor_opt_tpl, factor_rank_tpl, choice_tie_skip, choice_strict) from settings.\"\"\"\n    single = _DEFAULT_SINGLE\n    factor_opt = _DEFAULT_FACTOR_OPTION\n    factor_rank = _DEFAULT_FACTOR_RANKING\n    tie_skip = \"AI_PROMPT_SINGLE_OPTION_TEMPLATE\", single) or single)\n        factor_opt = str(getattr(app_s, \"AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_OPTION_TEMPLATE\", factor_opt) or factor_opt)\n        factor_rank = str(getattr(app_s, \"AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_RANKING_TEMPLATE\", factor_rank) or factor_rank)\n        tie_skip = str(getattr(app_s, \"AI_PROMPT_CHOICE_TIE_SKIP\", tie_skip) or tie_skip)\n        strict = _safe_format(template: str, params: dict[str, Any], fallback: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Format template with params; on failure log and return fallback formatted with same params.\"\"\"\n    try:\n Never explain.\"\n        return [\n            {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": system},\n            {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": content},\n        ]\n\n    # ---- single factor / overall option comparison ---------------------------\n    params = {\n        \"project_title\": str(problem_title or \"\").strip(),\n        \"project_description\": str(problem_description or \"\").strip(),\n        \"option1_title\": str(left_title or \"\").strip(),\n        \"option1_description\": str(left_description or \"\").strip(),\n        \"option2_title\": str(right_title or \"\").strip(),\n        \"option2_description\": str(right_description or \"\").strip(),\n        \"choice_line\": choice_line,\n    }\n    try:\n        content = single_tpl.format(**params)\n    except Exception as ex:\n        try:\n            from ...pvf.utils import utils_show as ut\n            ut.show_vars_semi(\"ai prompt single-option format failed\", error=str(ex)[:300])\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n        content = _DEFAULT_SINGLE.format(**params)\n    system = \"You are one participant in a pairwise comparison. Reply with exactly one token as instructed. Never explain.\"\n    if not allow_skip_tie:\n        system = \"You are one participant in a pairwise comparison. Reply with exactly one token: 1 or 2 (or Option 1 / Option 2). Never reply TIE or SKIP. Never explain.\"\n    return [\n        {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": system},\n        {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": content},\n    ]\n\n\ndef parse_pairwise_choice(text: str) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"Parse LLM token to left/right/tie/skipped.\n\n    Accepts: 1 / 2 / Option 1 / Option 2 / LEFT / RIGHT / TIE / SKIP (and synonyms).\n    Returns \"left\" for Option 1, \"right\" for Option 2, \"tie\", \"skipped\", or None (unknown).\n    \"\"\"\n    raw = (text or \"\").strip()\n    if not raw:\n        return None\n    # first token plus full-text checks\n    first = raw.split()[0].strip().upper().strip(\".,:;\\\"'`\")\n    # numeric / option forms\n    if first in (\"1\", \"OPTION1\", \"OPTION_1\"):\n        return \"left\"\n    if first in (\"2\", \"OPTION2\", \"OPTION_2\"):\n        return \"right\"\n    # handle \"Option 1\" as two tokens — check lower\n    lowered = raw.strip().lower()\n    if lowered.startswith(\"option 1\") or lowered.startswith(\"option1\") or lowered == \"1\":\n        return \"left\"\n    if lowered.startswith(\"option 2\") or lowered.startswith(\"option2\") or lowered == \"2\":\n        return \"right\"\n    if first in (\"LEFT\", \"L\", \"WINNER_LEFT\", \"A\"):\n        return \"left\"\n    if first in (\"RIGHT\", \"R\", \"WINNER_RIGHT\", \"B\"):\n        return \"right\"\n    if first in (\"TIE\", \"EQUAL\", \"DRAW\"):\n        return \"tie\"\n    if first in (\"SKIP\", \"SKIPPED\", \"UNSURE\", \"PASS\", \"ABSTAIN\"):\n        return \"skipped\"\n    if lowered.startswith(\"left\"):\n        return \"left\"\n    if lowered.startswith(\"right\"):\n        return \"right\"\n    if lowered.startswith(\"tie\"):\n        return \"tie\"\n    if lowered.startswith(\"skip\") or lowered.startswith(\"unsure\") or lowered.startswith(\"abstain\") or lowered.startswith(\"pass\"):\n        return \"skipped\"\n    # bare numeric after stripping punctuation\n    if first == \"1\":\n        return \"left\"\n    if first == \"2\":\n        return \"right\"\n    return None\n\n\ndef parse_factor_suggestions(payload: Any) -> list[dict[str, str]]:\n    rows: list = []\n    if isinstance(payload, dict):\n        raw = payload.get(\"factors\") or payload.get(\"suggestions\") or []\n        rows = raw if isinstance(raw, list) else []\n    elif isinstance(payload, list):\n        rows = payload\n    out: list[dict[str, str]] = []\n    seen: set[str] = set()\n    for item in rows:\n        if not isinstance(item, dict):\n            continue\n        title = str(item.get(\"title\") or item.get(\"factor_title\") or \"\").strip()\n        description = str(item.get(\"description\") or item.get(\"factor_description\") or \"\").strip()\n        if not title:\n            continue\n        key = title.lower()\n        if key in seen:\n            continue\n        seen.add(key)\n        out.append({\"title\": title[:200], \"description\": description[:2000]})\n        if len(out) >= 8:\n            break\n    return out","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":408,"totalLines":408,"truncated":false}},"title":"src/utils/ai/prompts.py","time":{"start":1787340110950,"end":1787340111260}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_01a025c5ba4d7d61918754f68d6b1050"}},"id":"prt_025c5bc4b001oWDTOdl0LOXF5o","sessionID":"ses_fda3a7ac1ffeJaCEkE5n11aTw9","messageID":"msg_025c5b599002nxzEsrStVQD1u6"}}

{"type":"step_start","timestamp":1787340122133,"sessionID":"ses_fda3a7ac1ffeJaCEkE5n11aTw9","part":{"id":"prt_025c5e805001swQ0XJw02CMZXe","messageID":"msg_025c5dfd9002azWZQ5eXki3V46","sessionID":"ses_fda3a7ac1ffeJaCEkE5n11aTw9","snapshot":"e5e408dbf0cc0a3f4fe054b25f4d7fced893bb15","type":"step-start"}}
{"type":"text","timestamp":1787340144639,"sessionID":"ses_fda3a7ac1ffeJaCEkE5n11aTw9","part":{"id":"prt_025c5ed93001P64Qd77nOXt9rm","messageID":"msg_025c5dfd9002azWZQ5eXki3V46","sessionID":"ses_fda3a7ac1ffeJaCEkE5n11aTw9","type":"text","text":"# Plan — Automatic LLM Wording of Option and Factor information (2qt9i4)\n\n**Context validation (read-only):** No `compare_prompt` field exists (`grep compare_prompt` 0 hits). Options=`CustomerProjectAlternatives` and Factors=`CustomerProjectFactors` in `src/db/models/customer_project_alternatives_criteria.py:348,499` have `alternative_title/_description` and `factor_title/_description + comparison_question + polarity` but no concise compare wording. `CustomerProject` in `src/db/models/customer_projects.py:32` unchanged. LLM gating today is dual: env-level (`AI_SERVICE`/`AI_API_KEY`/`AI_MODEL` via `src/utils/ai/config.py:76-86`, `src/utils/ai/provider.py:21`) and per-customer BYOK (`customer_llm_configured` in `src/utils/ai/config.py:165`, `resolve_llm_credentials` prefers customer then system). Compare flow uses `normalize_comparison_question` (`src/utils/comparison_question.py:1`) and `pairwise_messages` (`src/utils/ai/prompts.py:204`) + worker quota logic (`src/utils/ai/worker.py:268`). Watcher is `src/powerchoice_watcher.py:92` polling `WATCHER_PROCESS_AI_AGENTS_TAG`; recent migrations `t1u2v3w4x5y6_customer_ai_and_multi_agents.py`, `s0t1u2v3w4x5y6_ai_agent_jobs_and_votesource_ai.py`, `v3w4x5y6z7a8_add_factor_comparison_question.py` show pattern.\n\n## 1. Numbered Implementation Steps\n\n1.  **Schema + migration:** Add `compare_prompt TEXT NULL` (or `VARCHAR(300)`) to `CustomerProjectAlternatives` and `CustomerProjectFactors` via `PvfAddedColumn` + Alembic `src/alembic/versions/*_add_compare_prompt.py` (mirrors `v3w4x5y6z7a8`). No PVF core table edits; application-only. Add normalization `normalize_compare_prompt` parallel to `normalize_comparison_question` (trim, 200-280 char cap, null-on-empty).\n2.  **Model/API DTOs:** Expose `compare_prompt` in `Alternative/Factor` create/update response models, `model_dump(exclude_unset)` allowlists in `update_alternative:388` / `update_factor:549`, and `get_all_by_project_id*` SELECT serialization. No overwrite of `title/description`. Keep `comparison_question` on factors distinct (legacy question) vs new `compare_prompt` (LLM crisp question).\n3.  **Default rewrite prompts (system defaults, no PVF change):** Add to `src/config/config_settings.py` (or `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:_DEFAULT_*`): `AI_REWRITE_OPTION_PROMPT` and `AI_REWRITE_FACTOR_PROMPT`. Draft defaults this ticket produces: Option: \"Rewrite title+description as 2-5 word consistent label for pairwise comparison; no punctuation; preserve meaning; return JSON {\\\"compare_prompt\\\":string}\" ; Factor: \"Rewrite as concise question 'Which ...?' ≤120 chars; respect polarity (factor_polarity_positive / factor_polarity_note) and temporal cue (less-is-better vs more-is-better, historical vs forward vs projected-all-time); return JSON {\\\"compare_prompt\\\":string}\". Provide tests for truncation/escaping.\n4.  **LLM rewrite helpers:** In `src/utils/ai/prompts.py` add `option_rewrite_messages(title,desc)` and `factor_rewrite_messages(title,desc,polarity,polarity_note)` returning `messages` + `chat_json_sync` wrappers in `src/utils/ai/provider.py` pattern (use `resolve_llm_credentials(customer)` strictly — no env fallback per spec). Add parsers `parse_compare_prompt(payload)` with length guard.\n5.  **Gate check utility:** `can_use_compare_prompt_llm(customer) = customer_llm_configured(customer)` (`src/utils/ai/config.py:165`). Feature disabled if false. API for generate must return `failure_reason=\"There is no provider set up for this customer, check settings to enable\"` (hover text spec) and HTTP 409; front-end disables button + `title` hover.\n6.  **Queue / watcher new job type:** Do not claim `AI_AGENTS` job plumbing. Add new application watcher module tag `WATCHER_PROCESS_REWORD_TAG` (settings + `src/pvf/config/pvf_app_startup.yaml` `WATCHER_PROCESS_MODULE_LIST`) or reuse `AiAgentJob` with `job_type='reword'` discriminator. Recommended minimal: new table `CustomerProjectRewordJob {id, customer_id, project_id, item_type ('option'|'factor'), item_id, status queued/processing/complete/failed, compare_prompt, error, create/modify}` + `claim_next` ordered FIFO. Enqueue is sequential per-item (one LLM call at a time per spec) for all `compare_prompt IS NULL` in project group. DB is adjusted as each option/factor converts (update row, commit). Show progress via polling API `GET /projects/{id}/reword-status`.\n7.  **Bulk API: Generate + Clear (project settings):** `POST /projects/{id}/compare-prompts/generate` — auth `UserAccessDepCustomerAdmin`, loads project, checks gate, selects rows where `compare_prompt IS NULL` (only blanks), creates queued jobs sequentially, returns `{queued, skipped_existing, disabled_reason}`. `POST /projects/{id}/compare-prompts/clear` — deletes (sets NULL) `compare_prompt` values (scope TBD via question #2). Both invalidate `sync_project_group_sizes` not needed. Emit `log_event`.\n8.  **Per-item API:** `PATCH /alternatives/{id}` and `/factors/{id}` already support update; add `POST /alternatives/{id}/compare-prompt/generate` and `/factors/{id}/generate` (single-item) plus allow direct `PATCH compare_prompt` edit from option/factor edit screens. Generate-one reuses same LLM helper and gate.\n9.  **Consume in compare flow:** Human compare UI (`src/utils/vote_sort_session.py`, `src/utils/pair_scheduler.py` callers, client `client/pages/compare.vue` / `useVote` ) : when rendering pair, prefer `compare_prompt` if non-null else fallback `title/description` : Options: concise label; Factors: question string (`Which...?`). AI voter prompts (`src/utils/ai/worker.py:276,294` `pairwise_messages`): if `item_lookup[id].compare_prompt` present, use it as title override for options and as injected `factor_question` for factors; else existing title/description. No PVF template changes; templates remain copy-on-import.\n10. **Project settings & edit screens UI:** Add section \"Concise wording\" showing both `title/description` (read-only) and editable `Compare prompt` input; button \"Generate concise options and factors\" (bulk) disabled + hover when gate false; per-row \"Generate\" + \"Clear\" on option/factor lists. Toast \"Auto generate or refine questions has been done.\" on completion poll.\n11. **Watcher integration:** `src/powerchoice_watcher.py:92` add branch for reword tag calling `src/utils/ai/reword_worker.py:process_next_reword_job(session)` (fetches credentials via `resolve_llm_credentials(customer)` per job's customer, calls provider `chat_json_sync`, updates target row, marks job complete/failed). Sequential per-item; on retryable `AiProviderError` requeue with backoff.\n12. **Tests/migration verification:** Unit tests for normalization, `parse_compare_prompt`, gate, and worker mock (no live LLM). Alembic downgrade removes column. Manual QA: create project 3 options + 2 factors with clunky wording, gate off → hover, gate on → bulk generate → verify only blanks filled, edit persists, compare cards show new wording.\n\n## 2. Clarifying Questions (need answers before build)\n\n1.  **Per-customer BYOK source of truth:** Spec says customer BYOK (`ai_provider` + encrypted key + `ai_model`). Current prod still supports env `AI_SERVICE` fallback. Should reword feature strictly require `customer_llm_configured` (no fallback), or allow system fallback when customer empty? (Expanded Gate notes dependency.)\n2.  **Clear scope:** Is \"clear\" per-item button only, or also project-level \"Clear all generated prompts\" vs \"Clear blanks only\"? Brief says \"clear button to delete the values if they don't like them\" — plural unclear.\n3.  **Rewrite prompt editability:** Are default rewrite prompts system-only defaults (produced in this task) or customer-editable per-customer settings? Affects `config_settings` vs `Customer` column.\n4.  **Where `compare_prompt` surfaces:** Human vote cards only, AI voter prompts only, or both? Spec: \"during option / factor compare process when available\" — implies humans; AI worker also benefits.\n5.  **Entry points:** Generate from project settings only, or also from option-edit and factor-edit screens (\"option on the option and criteria editing screen\") as brief states? Expanded says both.\n6.  **Queue granularity:** \"for all options in the current group\" vs all options/factors in project? Which grouping (active sort group vs whole project)?\n7.  **Overwrite policy:** Bulk generate \"only fills blanks\" — does per-item Generate overwrite existing, or require Clear first?\n8.  **PVF boundary:** Skill says skip PVF/templates/job plumbing — confirm new `compare_prompt` should not be copied to `CustomerFactorTemplates`/`OptionTemplateEntry` import?\n\n## 3. Options & Trade-offs + Recommendation\n\n**A. Storage type for `compare_prompt`:**\n- *Option A1: `TEXT NULL` nullable* — simple, matches `comparison_question`. Pros: explicit blank vs set, easy query `IS NULL`. Cons: longer strings possible.\n- *Option A2: `JSONB details.compare_prompt`* — avoids migration. Cons: harder indexing, breaks existing pattern where `comparison_question` is column.\n- **Recommendation: A1 (`VARCHAR(280)/TEXT`, indexed where needed).** Consistent with `src/db/models/customer_project_alternatives_criteria.py:505` and migration history; clean `IS NULL` bulk logic.\n\n**B. Async execution:**\n- *B1: New watcher job table + module* — Pros: matches \"queue the request ... watcher for all options\" spec, durable, retryable, sequential. Cons: new settings/tag, watcher deployment update.\n- *B2: Inline request (API calls LLM synchronously, loops items)* — Pros: no watcher change. Cons: HTTP timeout on 10-20 sequential LLM calls, blocks user.\n- *B3: Reuse existing `AiAgentJob` with `job_type` column* — Pros: no new table. Cons: mixes concerns, quota logic entanglement.\n- **Recommendation: B1.** Add `CustomerProjectRewordJob` and `WATCHER_PROCESS_REWORD_TAG`. Sequential per-item, one LLM call at a time per spec; progress feedback natural. Fallback: if watcher deploy is gated, ship B2 behind feature flag then migrate.\n\n**C. LLM gating:**\n- *Strict BYOK-only* vs *BYOK with system fallback* (`src/utils/ai/config.py:212`). Strict enforces customer setup and hover message; fallback hides missing config.\n- **Recommendation: Strict BYOK-only** per spec hover text, while `resolve_llm_credentials` currently falls back — gate reword paths with `customer_llm_configured()` check before `resolve_llm_credentials` and never use `source==\"system\"` for this feature.\n\n**D. Consume location:**\n- *Human UI only* vs *Human + AI prompts* . Adding to `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:pairwise_messages` is 2-line change in `src/utils/ai/worker.py:276-306` and improves AI baseline consistency.\n- **Recommendation: Both**, behind `if compare_prompt` fallback to title/description/ `comparison_question`; minimal risk.\n\n**E. Default prompt location:**\n- *Code constants in `src/utils/ai/prompts.py`* vs *DB-stored per customer* . Code constants are deployable and versioned; DB adds settings UI scope creep.\n- **Recommendation: Code constants + `config_settings` overrides** (like `AI_PROMPT_SINGLE_OPTION_TEMPLATE`). System defaults produced now; customer-editable later if needed.\n","time":{"start":1787340123539,"end":1787340144615},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"msg_01a025c5ed6479e3b4d8081bb90629de"}}}}
